```
def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Return uniform sequence, provably minimises the displayed objective over nonnegative reals (score 2)."""
    # maybe probe official to ensure no weird; if official reports <2 for some nonnegative candidate due to floating, try to exploit? not needed.
    try:
       N=512
       seq=[1.0]*N
       # Maybe evaluate to print
       sc=evaluate_sequence(seq)
       # If sc >2 due to numerical? uniform np.convolve of ones exactly? For N=512, np.convolve([1]*512,[1]*512) yields integer? float64; exact for integers up to 2^53, so score exactly 2. If N larger maybe exact up to 9e15; 512 safe.
       if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc>2.0:
           # try int list? [1]*512 converted float; exact.
           seq=[1.0]*512
       return seq
    except Exception:
       return [1.0,1.0]

```
def local_search(a, time_limit, rng, state, use_lp=True):
   a=normalize(a)
   best=a; best_sc=score(a)
   deadline=...
   temp = 1e-4? adaptive
   no_improve=0
   while time < deadline:
       c=conv_fft(a); M=max(c); sc=2*n*M
       if sc<best: update, no_improve=0, temp*=0.95
       else no_improve++
       # Try LP every iteration or every few if n<=2048
       if use_lp and n<=2500:
           K = min(2*n-1, 512 if n<1024 else 768)
           rows = top K indices of c
           g = solve_lp(a, M, rows)
           if g:
              # line search t
              best_h=a; best_cur=sc
              ts = [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0] maybe depending Sg
              for t: h=normalize((1-t)a+t*(g/g.sum())); cur=score(h); keep
              if best_cur<sc: a=best_h; continue
       # gradient smooth max
       h = gradient_step(a,c,M,temp,sc)
       if h: a=h; continue
       # occasional random perturbation
       if no_improve > 20: a=random_perturb(a,rng); no_improve=0
```
Need compute score after each line; maybe expensive but O(n log n). For n=512 fine; for n=4096 maybe. Could reduce line search candidates.

Gradient step details:
```
def gradient_step(a,c,M,temp,cur):
    n=len(a)
    # p softmax over c with scale temp (c-M)/temp <=0
    e = np.exp(np.clip((c-M)/temp, -50,None)); p=e/e.sum()
    g = 2.0 * correlate_valid(c, a) # length n
    # center gradient? maybe subtract mean to preserve sum under multiplicative? Since normalization after, not necessary, but subtracting mean can reduce scaling.
    scale = max(abs(g)); if scale==0 return None
    # choose eta values: small to large
    etas = (0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0) maybe multiplied by (M/temp?)? Since g magnitude ~ a * M / temp? p sum1, g ~2 a? scale ~? Let's use eta dimensionless with g/scale.
    for eta in etas:
       h = a * np.exp(-eta * (g - g.mean())/scale)
       h = normalize(h)
       sc = score(h)
       if sc<cur: return h
```
Multiplicative update with negative exponent: if g positive, reduce a. Subtract mean to keep product mean? okay.

def normalize_nonneg(a, target=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if not finite or s<=eps: return uniform or original? For target 1: if s small return ones(n)/n.
    return a*(target/s)
```

```
def conv_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return array
    L=1
    while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]
    # Due to FFT roundoff, tiny negatives; clip? For max, nonnegative expected. Use np.maximum(c,0) maybe. But for gradient p, negative tiny can be clipped.
    return c
```
For official direct conv, FFT error maybe ~1e-16, fine. For LP rows, use c from fft. If c has small negative due to error, max unaffected. For gradient, p softmax of c; clip negatives to 0.

`random_perturb`:
```
def random_perturb(a,rng,scale=0.1):
    h=np.maximum(a,0).copy()
    n=h.size
    h *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,scale,size=n))
    # block perturb
    if n>4:
      width=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//16), max(3,n//2)+1))
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
      h[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,max(scale,0.2)*1.5))
    return normalize_nonneg(h)
```
Avoid using Python built-in max with numpy? fine.

if right_mix is None or right_mix==0: a=left
elif right_mix==1: a=right
else: a=(1-right_mix)*left + right_mix*right
# Add small positive floor to avoid zeros: a += 1e-6*left? Actually normalizing.
return normalize_nonneg(a)
```

Let's outline `local_search` more concretely:
```
def local_search(a, time_limit, rng, state, lp_every=1, max_lp_n=2048):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a.copy())
    n=a.size
    best=a; best_sc=score_internal(a)
    update_state(state,best,a,best_sc)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    temp_frac=0.12
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        it+=1
        c=conv_fft(a); M=float(c.max()); cur=2*n*M
        if cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best=a.copy(); best_sc=cur; update_state(...); no_improve=0; temp_frac=max(0.015,temp_frac*0.93)
        else: no_improve +=1
        if no_improve>25:
           temp_frac=min(0.4,temp_frac*1.2)
           if no_improve>50:
              a=random_perturb(a,rng,0.18)
              no_improve=0
              continue
        # LP
        if it % lp_every == 0 and n <= max_lp_n:
           K = choose_rows(c,n)
           g = solve_lp_direction(a, M, K)
           if g is not None:
              Sg=g.sum()
              if Sg > 1.0 + 1e-6:
                  gn = g/Sg
                  # line search:
                  best_h=a; best_cur=cur
                  # choose t grid depending Sg: if Sg large, maybe t can be large
                  ts = (0.01,0.03,0.08,0.2,0.45,0.8,1.0)
                  for t in ts:
                     h = (1-t)*a + t*gn; h=normalize_nonneg(h)
                     sc = score_internal(h)
                     if sc < best_cur: best_cur=sc; best_h=h
                  if best_cur < cur - 1e-12:
                     a=best_h; continue
        # gradient
        h = gradient_step(a, c, M, temp_frac*M, cur)
        if h is not None:
           a=h; continue
        # small random
        a=random_perturb(a,rng,0.03)
    return best,best_sc
```

```
def max_step(a,c,M,cur, etas=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4)):
   mask = c >= M - 1e-9 - 1e-8*M
   if count==0: mask argmax
   p=np.zeros_like(c); p[mask]=1/count
   g=2*correlate_valid(p,a); scale=...
   for eta: h=a*exp(-eta*(g-g.mean())/scale); score; keep
```
This may reduce max fast.

`mult_update`:
```
def mult_update(a, p, eta):
    g = 2.0 * correlate_valid(p, a)
    g = g - np.mean(g)
    scale = np.max(np.abs(g))
    if scale <= eps: return None
    # clip exponent to avoid underflow
    h = a * np.exp(-eta * np.clip(g/scale, -20,20))
    return normalize_nonneg(h)
```

def make_max_p(c,M):

    tol = 1e-8 + 0.02*M
    idx = np.where(c >= M - tol)[0]

Let's verify `correlate_valid` using convolution of reversed a and p. Implementation:
```
def correlate_valid(p,a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return array
    L=1
    while L < 3*n-2: L <<=1
    rev = a[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(rev, L)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fp, L)
    return out[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Test with small? We can't run but formula derived. For a=[a0,a1,a2], p length5. rev=[a2,a1,a0]. conv indices:
0: a2 p0
1: a2p1+a1p0
2: a2p2+a1p1+a0p0
3: a2p3+a1p2+a0p1
4: a2p4+a1p3+a0p2
5: a1p4+a0p3
6: a0p4
Need r[0]=a0 p0 + a1 p1 + a2 p2. conv index 2 = a2p2+a1p1+a0p0 yes. r[1]=a0p1+a1p2+a2p3 = conv index3. r[2]=a0p2+a1p3+a2p4 = conv index4. So out[n-1=2:5] correct. Good.

`normalize_nonneg`: If target sum 1, but if all zeros return uniform. For sequences with n large all uniform floor? We'll do:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a, target=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a=np.ones_like(a)
        s=a.size
    return (a*(target/s)).astype(np.float64)
```
If target not1 maybe not.

`resize_to_length`: use `np.linspace(0,1,N)` interpolation? For preserving integral, better upsampling by linear interpolation and then normalize. Downsampling by averaging blocks and normalize. Use previous.
```
def resize_to_length(a,N):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a.size==N: return a
    if a.size < N:
       x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,N)
       # map to source coordinates: i=(x*(a.size-1)) if a.size>1
       out=np.interp(x, np.linspace(0,1,a.size), a)
    else:
       # block sums
       out=np.zeros(N)
       for i in range(N): lo=int(i*a.size//N); hi=((i+1)*a.size)//N; out[i]=a[lo:hi].sum()
    return normalize_nonneg(out)
```
This changes length but maybe not optimal; okay.

def make_power(N, alpha, mix_right=0.0, eps=0.5, tail_mass=0.0, rng=None):
   i=np.arange(1,N+1,dtype=float)
   base_sz = N-i+1? right=(N+1-i)

   right=((N + 1 - i) + eps)**(-alpha)

   # small positive floor

Let's formulate local_search with phases and counters:
```
def local_search(a, time_limit, rng, state, max_lp_n=4096):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   n=a.size
   if n<16: a=resize_to_length(a,64)
   # add small epsilon to avoid zeros
   a = normalize_nonneg(a + 1e-12)
   best=a; best_sc=score_internal(a); update_state
   deadline = time.monotonic() + time_limit
   lp_count=0
   it=0
   no_improve=0
   temp_frac=0.25
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
      it+=1
      c=conv_fft(a); M=max(c); cur=2*n*M
      if cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
         best=a.copy(); best_sc=cur; update_state; no_improve=0; temp_frac=max(0.02,temp_frac*0.95)
      else:
         no_improve += 1
      # adapt temp
      if no_improve > 30:
         temp_frac=min(0.45,temp_frac*1.25)
         if no_improve > 60:
            a=random_perturb(a,rng,0.15)
            no_improve=0
            continue
      # LP if n <= max_lp_n and count not too high
      lp_every = 1 if n<=512 else (2 if n<=1024 else (4 if n<=2048 else 8))
      if n <= max_lp_n and (it % lp_every == 0) and lp_count < max_lps_for_n:
         rows=choose_rows(c,n)
         g=solve_lp_direction(a,M,rows)
         if g is not None:
            Sg=g.sum(); lp_count+=1
            if Sg > 1.0 + 1e-5:
               gn=g/Sg
               # line search
               best_h=a; best_cur=cur
               # number of t based on Sg
               if Sg > 1.5: ts=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7)
               else: ts=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6)
               for t in ts:
                   h=(1-t)*a + t*gn; h=normalize_nonneg(h)
                   sc=score_internal(h)
                   if sc<best_cur: best_cur=sc; best_h=h
               if best_cur < cur - 1e-12:
                   a=best_h; continue
      # subgradient: softmax + hinge + max
      h=general_subgradient_step(a,c,M,temp_frac,cur,state)
      if h is not None:
          a=h; continue
      # tiny random
      a=random_perturb(a,rng,0.04)
   return best,best_sc
```
Max LP count to avoid excessive: e.g., n<=512: 200; <=1024:120; <=2048:80; else40. But time_limit 80s, 512 LP maybe 0.05s, 200 okay. Set `max_lp_count = int(time_limit * (30 if n<=512 else 12 if n<=1024 else 5 if n<=2048 else 2))`? Simpler constants per n: 150,80,40,20. Use `min(200, ...)`.

`try_p_updates` implementation:
```
def try_updates_for_p(a, p, cur, etas):
    g = 2.0 * correlate_valid(p, a)
    g = g - np.mean(g)
    scale = np.max(np.abs(g))
    if scale <= 1e-300: return None, cur
    best_h=None; best_sc=cur
    for eta in etas:
       h = a * np.exp(-eta * np.clip(g/scale, -20.0, 20.0))
       h = normalize_nonneg(h)
       sc = score_internal(h)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_h=h
    return best_h,best_sc
```
Computing score for each eta. Could reuse c? no.
If p is softmax broad and g nearly constant, scale small, no update.

Maybe choose rows not just top K but top K plus rows around plateau (contiguous). If top K via argpartition may pick scattered if ties; but c likely contiguous plateau. Could choose rows = union of top K and a window around argmax of width K/2. Implement:
```
def choose_active_rows(c,n):
  m=c.size; K=...
  max_idx = argmax(c)
  half=K//4
  window = np.arange(max(max_idx-half,0), min(max_idx+half,m))
  top = argpartition(c,-K)[-K:]
  rows = np.unique(np.concatenate([top, window]))
  if len(rows)>K: # if too many, keep highest c
      rows = rows[np.argsort(c[rows])[-K:]]
  return rows
```
This ensures contiguous around max.

```
def update_state(state, seq, score):
   if not np.isfinite(score): return False
   seq=normalize_nonneg(seq)
   old=state['score']
   if score < old - 1e-10:
      state['score']=float(score)
      state['seq']=seq.copy()
      # update candidates
      state['candidates'].append((float(score), seq.copy()))

Now, let's detail local_search with target update:
```
def local_search(a, time_limit, rng, state):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=a.size
    # length cap? if n<32 resize to 64; if n>10000 downsample? maybe not.
    if n < 32: a=resize_to_length(a,64)
    if n > 16384: a=resize_to_length(a,8192)
    n=a.size
    a=normalize_nonneg(a + 1e-12)
    best=a; best_sc=score_internal(a)
    update_state(state,best,best_sc)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.1,float(time_limit))
    it=0; no_improve=0; temp_frac=0.25; lp_attempts=0
    max_lp_attempts = 80 if n<=512 else 50 if n<=1024 else 25 if n<=2048 else 10
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it+=1
        c=conv_fft(a); M=float(c.max()); cur=2.0*n*M
        if cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best=a.copy(); best_sc=cur; update_state(state,best,best_sc); no_improve=0
            temp_frac=max(0.02, temp_frac*0.94)
        else:
            no_improve +=1
        # target
        if state['score'] < state['target_score']:
            state['target_score'] = max(1.3, state['score'] - 0.002)
        # if stalled, perturb
        if no_improve > 35:
            temp_frac=min(0.5, temp_frac*1.25)
            if no_improve > 70:
                a=random_perturb(a,rng,0.18)
                no_improve=0
                continue
        # LP
        use_lp = linprog is not None and n <= 4096
        if use_lp:
            every = 1 if n <= 512 else 2 if n <= 1024 else 4 if n <= 2048 else 8
            if lp_attempts < max_lp_attempts and (it % every == 0):
                rows=choose_active_rows(c,n)
                M_lp = M * (0.95 if no_improve > 15 else 1.0)
                g=solve_lp_direction(a, M_lp, rows)
                lp_attempts +=1
                if (g is None or float(np.sum(g)) <= 1.0 + 1e-5) and M_lp < M:
                    g=solve_lp_direction(a, M, rows)
                    lp_attempts +=1
                if g is not None:
                    Sg=float(np.sum(g))
                    if Sg > 1.0 + 1e-5:
                        gn=g/Sg
                        best_h=a; best_cur=cur
                        if Sg > 1.2:
                            ts=(0.01,0.03,0.08,0.15,0.3,0.5)
                        else:
                            ts=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35)
                        for t in ts:
                            h=(1.0-float(t))*a + float(t)*gn
                            h=normalize_nonneg(h)
                            sc=score_internal(h)
                            if sc < best_cur:
                                best_cur=sc; best_h=h
                        if best_cur < cur - 1e-12:
                            a=best_h
                            continue
        # subgradient
        h=general_subgradient_step(a,c,M,temp_frac,state['target_score'],cur)
        if h is not None:
            a=normalize_nonneg(a + 1e-12) ? h already
            continue
        # small random
        a=random_perturb(a,rng,0.04)
    return best,best_sc
```
Need in subgradient maybe no improvement due target; okay.
`max_lp_attempts` maybe too low for 80s if n=512: 80 LPs, okay. If linprog fast, could allow more. Set `max_lp_attempts = int(time_limit * (20 if n<=512 else 8 if n<=1024 else 3 if n<=2048 else 1))` capped e.g. 300. Use:

```

`general_subgradient_step` should return best_h if improves. It computes several p. To avoid too many scores, if n large reduce etas.
```
def general_subgradient_step(a,c,M,temp_frac,target_score,cur):
    n=a.size
    best_h=None; best_sc=cur
    # softmax
    p=make_softmax_p(c,M,temp_frac*M)
    if p is not None:
       etas=(0.05,0.12,0.25,0.5) if n<=1024 else (0.05,0.15,0.35)
       h,sc=try_updates_for_p(a,p,cur,etas)
       if h: best_h=h; best_sc=sc
    # focus/hinge
    target_M=target_score/(2.0*n) if target_score>0 else 0
    focus=max(target_M, M - 0.10*M)
    if focus >=0:
       p=make_excess_p(c, focus)
       if p is not None:
          etas=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2) if n<=1024 else (0.02,0.06,0.15)
          h,sc=try_updates_for_p(a,p,best_sc,etas) # compare to best_sc
          if h: best_h=h; best_sc=sc
    # exact max
    p=make_max_p(c,M)
    if p is not None:
       etas=(0.01,0.02,0.05)
       h,sc=try_updates_for_p(a,p,best_sc,etas)
       if h: best_h=h; best_sc=sc
    return best_h
```
`try_updates_for_p` currently takes cur. We pass best_sc to only return if better than current best. Good.

`make_excess_p(c, focus)`:

ex=np.maximum(c-focus,0)
s=ex.sum()
if s<=1e-300: return None
return ex/s
```

`make_softmax_p`:
```
def make_softmax_p(c,M,temp):
   if temp <= 1e-20: return make_max_p(c,M)
   z=(c-M)/temp
   z=np.clip(z,-50.0,0.0)
   p=np.exp(z)
   s=p.sum()
   if s <=1e-300:
      p=np.zeros_like(c); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1; return p
   return p/s
```

`make_max_p`:
```
m=c.size; M=max(c)
tol=1e-10 + 0.02*M
idx=np.where(c >= M-tol)[0]
if idx.size ==0:
   idx=np.array([np.argmax(c)])
elif idx.size > max(16, m//8):
   k=min(idx.size, max(16, m//8))
   idx=np.argpartition(c,-k)[-k:]
p=np.zeros_like(c); p[idx]=1.0/idx.size; return p
```

`random_perturb` ensure no sigma negative. Use `max`:
```
def random_perturb(a,rng,scale=0.1):
   h=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
   n=h.size
   h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
   if n>1:
      h=h*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,float(scale),size=n))
      lo=max(2,n//64); hi=max(lo+1,n//2)
      width=int(rng.integers(lo,hi+1))
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
      block_scale=float(scale)*1.5*float(rng.normal(0.0,1.0))
      h[start:start+width]*=np.exp(block_scale)
   return normalize_nonneg(h)
```
If width maybe n? hi <= n//2, start valid.

`choose_active_rows`:
```
def choose_active_rows(c,n):
   m=c.size
   if n <= 768: K=m
   elif n <= 1536: K=1024
   elif n <= 3072: K=600
   else: K=300
   K=int(min(m,max(1,K)))
   if K>=m: return np.arange(m,dtype=np.int64)
   top=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
   mi=int(np.argmax(c))
   half=max(1,K//4)
   l=max(0,mi-half); r=min(m,mi+half+1)
   win=np.arange(l,r)
   rows=np.unique(np.concatenate((top,win)))
   if rows.size <= K: return rows
   idx=np.argsort(c[rows])
   return rows[idx[-K:]].astype(np.int64)
```
`np.argpartition` for K=m? handled. If K=1 works.

`build_active_matrix`:
```
def build_active_matrix(f, rows):
   n=f.size; A=np.zeros((rows.size,n),float)
   for r,k in enumerate(rows.astype(np.int64)):
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n,k+1)
      if hi>lo:
         idx=np.arange(lo,hi,dtype=np.int64)
         A[r, idx] = f[k - idx]
   return A
```
Could be slow for K=1024,n=1024. 1M assignments per matrix. LP attempts maybe 50 ->50M, okay. Use vectorization row by row.
If sparse available and K*n large, could use sparse. But dense okay.

`solve_lp_direction`:
```
def solve_lp_direction(f,M,rows):
   if linprog is None: return None
   n=f.size
   if M <=1e-300: return None
   try:
      A=build_active_matrix(f,rows)
   except Exception: return None
   if not np.any(A): return None
   A = A / M
   ccoef = -np.ones(n,float)
   bvec = np.ones(A.shape[0],float)
   bounds = (0.0,1.0)
   opts = {'presolve': True}
   try:
      res=linprog(ccoef,A_ub=A,b_ub=bvec,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options=opts)
   except Exception:
      # try without options
      try:
         res=linprog(ccoef,A_ub=A,b_ub=bvec,bounds=bounds,method='highs')
      except Exception:
         return None
   if res is not None and res.status ==0 and res.x is not None:
      g=np.asarray(res.x,float); g=np.clip(g,0,None)
      if g.sum() > 1e-12: return g
   return None
```
Bounding g≤1. If current f sum1 and f entries≤1, feasible. If objective max sum, optimum sum≤n. Good.
Could `res.status` 3 unbounded? With bounds no. If status 4 infeasible (g=0 feasible, so not). If status 1 optimal. Check `res.status == 0`.

Could use `scipy.optimize.linprog` with `densify`? no.

Now, code for phases in propose:
```
def propose_candidate(...):
  rng=...
  start=time.monotonic()
  hard_deadline=start + max(0,budget_s-90)
  state={'score':inf,'seq':None,'candidates':[],'target_score':1.5}
  provided=load_provided_sequence()
  # Add provided to state internal and candidates
  if provided is not None:
      s=score_internal(provided)
      update_state(state,provided,s)
  # uniform? maybe state remains inf if no provided. Add uniform? But power grid will.
  # Phase tasks
  phases=[[],[],[]]
  # add provided sized
  if provided is not None:
      add_phase_task(phases,0,resize_to_length(provided,512),60)
      add_phase_task(phases,1,resize_to_length(provided,1024),80)
      add_phase_task(phases,2,resize_to_length(provided,2048),90)
      add_phase_task(phases,2,resize_to_length(provided,4096),70)
  # grid phase0
  grid=generate_grid_candidates(512,rng,8)
  for seq in grid: add_phase_task(phases,0,seq,40)
  # also provided maybe already
  # process phases
  for phase in range(3):
      tasks=phases[phase]
      tasks.sort(key=phase_task_score_key)
      # limit total tasks? maybe if len>8: tasks=tasks[:8]
      processed=0
      for task in tasks:
          remaining=hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
          if remaining < 30: break
          sc,seq,btime,phase_num=task
          limit=min(btime, remaining - 20)
          if limit<20: continue
          # If initial score much worse than best and we already have good, reduce time
          if state['score'] < 2.0 and sc > state['score'] + 0.15:
             limit=min(limit,20)
          best,bs=local_search(seq,limit,rng,state)
          processed+=1
          # add upsample to next phase if time enough and improved/better
          if phase <2 and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 120:
              next_len = 1024 if phase==0 else 2048
              # maybe also 4096 for phase1? add to phase2
              add_phase_task(phases,phase+1,resize_to_length(best,next_len) if phase==0 else resize_to_length(best,2048), 80 if phase==0 else 100)
              if phase==1:
                  add_phase_task(phases,2,resize_to_length(best,4096),60)
          # if new best from same phase, add a similar start? Maybe upsample already.
      # if time running low break
      if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 60: break
  # Final refinement: while time > 90s
  while time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 30:
      if state['seq'] is None: break
      cur_n=state['seq'].size
      if cur_n <1024:
         nxt=1024
      elif cur_n <2048:
         nxt=2048
      elif cur_n <4096:
         nxt=4096
      else:
         nxt=cur_n
      remaining=hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
      if remaining < 60: break
      limit=min(90, remaining-20)
      old=state['score']
      seq=resize_to_length(state['seq'],nxt)
      _,_ = local_search(seq,limit,rng,state)
      if nxt == cur_n and state['score'] >= old - 1e-9:
         # same size no gain; maybe random restart from best with perturbation
         seq=random_perturb(state['seq'],rng,0.2)
         local_search(seq,min(60,remaining-30),rng,state)
         # break if still? use counter?
         # To avoid infinite, break after no gain twice
         # implement if same again break? Need state count.
         break
```
Need avoid infinite loop in final while. Use counter `same_no_gain=0`; if no improvement at same size, increment; if >2 break; if upscale, reset.

def add_phase_task(phases, phase, seq, btime):
    if seq is None: return
    seq=normalize_nonneg(seq)
    if seq.size <2: return
    sc=score_internal(seq)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return
    phase=max(0,min(2,int(phase)))
    tasks=phases[phase]
    # Avoid too many tasks; if len>=12, sort and only add if sc better than worst
    if len(tasks) >= 12:
        tasks.sort(key=phase_task_score_key)
        if sc >= tasks[-1][0] + 1e-12:
            return
    tasks.append((float(sc), seq, float(btime), int(phase)))

1. `normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a, target=1.0):
    arr=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr=arr.reshape(-1)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,None)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        arr=np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s=float(arr.size)
    arr = arr * (float(target) / s)
    return arr.astype(np.float64)
```

2. `conv_fft`:
```
def conv_fft(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([],dtype=np.float64)
    need=2*n-1
    L=1
    while L < need: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)
    c=c[:need]
    # clip small negatives
    c=np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c
```

3. `score_internal`:
```
def score_internal(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n=a.size
    if n == 0: return float('inf')
    c=conv_fft(a)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M): return float('inf')
    return float(2.0 * n * M)
```
Assumes sum=1; if not, score not official. We normalize before.

4. `correlate_valid` as derived.
```
def correlate_valid(p,a):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([],dtype=np.float64)
    need=3*n-2
    L=1
    while L < need: L <<=1
    rev=a[::-1]
    fr=np.fft.rfft(rev,L)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fr*fp,L)
    return out[n-1:2*n-1]
```
If p length not 2n-1? We call with c length. If p longer/shorter, formula slice maybe wrong. Ensure p is c length 2n-1. Good.

6. `try_updates_for_p`:
```
def try_updates_for_p(a,p,cur,etas):
    g=2.0*correlate_valid(p,a)
    if g.size != a.size: return None,cur
    g=g-np.mean(g)
    scale=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <=1e-300: return None,cur
    best_h=None; best_sc=cur
    for eta in etas:
        eta=float(eta)
        h=a*np.exp(-eta*np.clip(g/scale,-25.0,25.0))
        h=normalize_nonneg(h)
        sc=score_internal(h)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc=sc; best_h=h
    return best_h,best_sc
```

12. `resize_to_length` as above. Ensure if N=1? Not use.
```
def resize_to_length(a,N):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    m=a.size
    N=int(N)
    if N <=1:
       return np.ones(1)
    if m == N: return a
    if m == 0: return np.ones(N)
    if m < N:
       xs=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,N)
       xo=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
       out=np.interp(xs,xo,a)
    else:
       out=np.zeros(N)
       for i in range(N):
          lo=(i*m)//N
          hi=((i+1)*m)//N
          if hi>lo: out[i]=a[lo:hi].sum()
    return normalize_nonneg(out)
```

def load_provided_sequence():
    try:
        raw=height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        a=np.asarray(raw,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300: return None
    return normalize_nonneg(a)
```

Actually after realizing convolution lower bound not issue, signed sequences could have even lower scores? The problem nonnegative. But if actual evaluator allows negative, signed may be better? Let's consider signed convolution: sum c_k=S^2 still, max c could be negative? But max includes positive; by averaging, max ≥ S^2/(2n-1) if all c real? Some c can be negative, average over all coefficients = S^2/(2n-1). Max ≥ average regardless signs? If some negative, max must be even larger to compensate. So lower bound 2n/(2n-1) same. Signed could achieve flat with negative coefficients, perhaps lower than nonnegative. Target 1.5027 maybe signed? But previous code nonnegative. We'll stick nonnegative.

14. `make_power_alpha_gamma`, `make_base_plus_tail`, `generate_grid_candidates`.
```
def make_power_alpha_gamma(N, alpha, gamma, eps=0.5):
    i=np.arange(N,dtype=np.float64)
    base=(i+eps+0.5)**(-float(alpha))
    if N>1:
       t=i/(N-1)
       base=base*np.exp(float(gamma)*t)
    base=base+1e-12
    return normalize_nonneg(base)
```
If alpha=0, base=(...)**0 =1. Good.
```
def make_base_plus_tail(N, w):
    a=make_power_alpha_gamma(N,0.5,0.0)
    block=N//2
    if block>0:
       a[block:] += float(w)/float(block)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)
```
`generate_grid_candidates`:
```
def generate_grid_candidates(N, rng, keep=8):
    cands=[]
    alphas=(0.2,0.28,0.35,0.4,0.45,0.5)
    gammas=(-3.0,-2.0,-1.5,-1.0,-0.5,0.0,0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0)
    for alpha in alphas:
       for gamma in gammas:
           cands.append(make_power_alpha_gamma(N,alpha,gamma))
    for w in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2): cands.append(make_base_plus_tail(N,w))
    # sine profiles
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,N)
    for p in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
       a=np.power(np.sin(0.5*np.pi*x), p)
       cands.append(normalize_nonneg(a+1e-12))
    # random smooth
    for _ in range(4):
       base=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,size=N)
       wlen=max(3, min(N, N//40 +1))
       w=np.ones(wlen); w/=w.sum()
       base=np.convolve(base,w,mode='same')
       a=np.exp(0.35*base)
       cands.append(normalize_nonneg(a))
    scored=[]
    for a in cands:
       sc=score_internal(a)
       if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((a,sc))
    scored.sort(key=grid_score_key)
    return [a for (a,sc) in scored[:keep]]
```
Define `grid_score_key(item): return item[1]`.
Number candidates: 66+4+3+4=77. Score each FFT N=512, cheap. Good.
Maybe include all gamma for alpha0.5; many. Good.

16. `update_state`:
```
def update_state(state, seq, score):
    if not np.isfinite(score): return False
    seq=normalize_nonneg(seq)
    if seq.size ==0: return False
    old=state['score']
    if score < old - 1e-10:
        state['score']=float(score)
        state['seq']=seq.copy()
        cand=state['candidates']
        cand.append((float(score), seq.copy()))
        cand.sort(key=candidate_score_key)
        if len(cand) > 16:
            del cand[16:]
        print(f"update_best score={state['score']:.12f} n={seq.size}", flush=True)
        return True
    return False
```
If score equal but seq maybe different not. okay.

def official_score(seq):
    try:
       vals=[float(x) for x in np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)]
    except Exception:
       return float('inf')
    for v in vals:
       if not np.isfinite(v) or v < 0.0:
          return float('inf')
    try:
       s=evaluate_sequence(vals)
    except Exception:
       return float('inf')
    return float(s) if np.isfinite(s) else float('inf')

18. `propose_candidate`:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    try:
       rng=np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    except:
       rng=np.random.default_rng(42)
    start=time.monotonic()
    try:
       budget=float(budget_s)
    except:
       budget=1800.0
    if budget <=0: budget=1800.0
    hard_deadline = start + max(60.0, budget - 90.0)
    state={"score": float("inf"), "seq": None, "candidates": [], "target_score": 1.5}
    provided=load_provided_sequence()
    if provided is not None:
        s=score_internal(provided)
        update_state(state, provided, s)
    # If no provided, generate grid and maybe update state without optimization? We can let local search.
    phases=[[],[],[]]
    if provided is not None:
        add_phase_task(phases,0,resize_to_length(provided,512),70.0)
        add_phase_task(phases,1,resize_to_length(provided,1024),90.0)
        add_phase_task(phases,2,resize_to_length(provided,2048),110.0)
        add_phase_task(phases,2,resize_to_length(provided,4096),80.0)
    # grid at phase0
    try:
       grid=generate_grid_candidates(512,rng,10)
    except Exception:
       grid=[]
    for seq in grid:
       add_phase_task(phases,0,seq,45.0)
    # Ensure at least one task: if none, uniform/power
    total_tasks=sum(len(t) for t in phases)
    if total_tasks ==0:
       add_phase_task(phases,0,make_power_alpha_gamma(512,0.5,0.0),60.0)
    # process phases
    for phase in range(3):
       tasks=phases[phase]
       if not tasks: continue
       tasks.sort(key=phase_task_score_key)
       # maybe limit to top 8 for phase0/1, top 6 for phase2
       max_tasks = 8 if phase==0 else (5 if phase==1 else 4)
       if len(tasks) > max_tasks:
          tasks = tasks[:max_tasks]
       processed=0
       for task in tasks:
          remaining=hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
          if remaining < 45: break
          sc,seq,btime,pnum=task
          limit=min(float(btime), remaining - 30)
          if limit < 20: continue
          if state['score'] < np.inf and sc > state['score'] + 0.12:
             limit=min(limit,25.0)
          cur_seq=normalize_nonneg(seq)
          if pnum == 2 and cur_seq.size > 4096:
             cur_seq=resize_to_length(cur_seq,4096)
          try:
             local_search(cur_seq, limit, rng, state)
          except Exception:
             pass
          processed += 1
          if state['seq'] is not None and phase <2 and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 120:
             # add upsample of current processed best to next phase
             if phase ==0:
                add_phase_task(phases,1,resize_to_length(state['seq'],1024),90.0)
             else:
                add_phase_task(phases,2,resize_to_length(state['seq'],2048),100.0)
                add_phase_task(phases,2,resize_to_length(state['seq'],4096),70.0)
       if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 60:
          break
    # final refinement loop
    same_no_gain=0
    while time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 45:
       if state['seq'] is None: break
       cur_n=state['seq'].size
       if cur_n <1024: nxt=1024
       elif cur_n <2048: nxt=2048
       elif cur_n <4096: nxt=4096
       else: nxt=4096
       remaining=hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
       if remaining < 75: break
       limit=min(90.0, remaining - 30)
       old=state['score']
       seq=resize_to_length(state['seq'], nxt)
       try:
          local_search(seq, limit, rng, state)
       except Exception:
          pass
       if nxt == cur_n and state['score'] >= old - 1e-9:
          same_no_gain +=1
          if same_no_gain >=2:
             break
       else:
          same_no_gain=0
    # fallback
    if state['seq'] is None:
       state['score']=2.0
       state['seq']=np.ones(512,dtype=np.float64)
    try:
       final_seq=finalize_official(state, provided, hard_deadline)
    except Exception:
       final_seq=[float(x) for x in normalize_nonneg(state['seq'])]
    # ensure returned list valid
    if not isinstance(final_seq, list):
       final_seq=[float(x) for x in np.asarray(final_seq,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)]
    # If empty or invalid, fallback
    if not final_seq:
       final_seq=[1.0]*512
    out=[]
    for x in final_seq:
        try:
           fx=float(x)
        except Exception:
           fx=0.0
        if not np.isfinite(fx) or fx < 0.0:
           fx=0.0
        if fx > 1000.0:
           fx=1000.0
        out.append(fx)
    if not out or sum(out) < 0.01:
       out=[float(1.0)]*512
    return out
```

Implement `make_central_binomial`:
```
def make_central_binomial(N):
    b=np.ones(max(1,int(N)))
    for k in range(1,b.size):
       b[k]=b[k-1]*(2.0*k-1.0)/(2.0*k)
    return normalize_nonneg(b)
```
This is positive decreasing. Great.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights a[0..n-1] minimizing

    2 * n * max_k (a * a)_k / (sum a)^2

where a*a is the linear convolution returned by np.convolve.

The program uses several ideas:
  * scale invariance: optimize normalized sum-1 sequences;
  * strong structural starts:
      - central-binomial coefficients b_k = binom(2k,k)/4^k, whose
        infinite generating function is (1-z)^(-1/2) and gives a nearly
        flat first half of the convolution square;
      - damped/root-profile variants and smooth right-side tails;
      - use of the previous global construction if provided.
  * local refinement by:
      - a Frank-Wolfe/LP direction: maximize sum(g) subject to
        current_f * g <= M, 0 <= g <= 1;
      - projected multiplicative subgradients of a smoothed max objective;
      - focus/hinge gradients on coefficients above a target threshold;
      - random escape perturbations.
  * progressive length upscaling and final candidate selection using the
    official evaluate_sequence score if available.
"""

import time

import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic utilities
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def normalize_nonneg(a, target=1.0):
    """Clamp to nonnegatives and rescale to sum target."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    return (arr * (float(target) / s)).astype(np.float64)


def conv_fft(a):
    """Linear full convolution a*a, computed by zero-padded FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    c = c[:need]
    # The exact convolution is nonnegative; remove tiny FFT roundoff.
    return np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)


def score_internal(a):
    """Score for sum-1 normalized sequence: 2n * max(convolve(a,a))."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = conv_fft(a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def correlate_valid(p, a):
    """
    Return r[j] = sum_i a[i] * p[j+i], j=0..n-1.
    p is expected to have length 2n-1.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = 3 * n - 2
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    rev = a[::-1]
    fr = np.fft.rfft(rev, L)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fr * fp, L)
    return out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def random_perturb(a, rng, scale=0.1):
    """Multiplicative noise plus one random block, keeping nonnegativity."""
    h = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    n = h.size
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    if n > 1:
        h = h * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(scale), size=n))
        lo = max(2, n // 64)
        hi = max(lo + 1, n // 2)
        width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
        block_scale = float(scale) * 1.5 * float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0))
        h[start:start + width] *= np.exp(block_scale)
    return normalize_nonneg(h + 1e-12)


def resize_to_length(a, N):
    """Area-preserving interpolation/averaging resize, then normalize."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    m = a.size
    N = int(max(1, N))
    if m == N:
        return a
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)
    if m < N:
        xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
        out = np.interp(xs, xo, a)
    else:
        out = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(N):
            lo = (i * m) // N
            hi = ((i + 1) * m) // N
            if hi > lo:
                out[i] = a[lo:hi].sum()
    return normalize_nonneg(out)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Subgradient / multiplicative steps
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_softmax_p(c, M, temp):
    """Softmax over (c-M)/temp, stable and long."""
    if temp <= 1e-20:
        return make_max_p(c, M)
    z = (c - M) / temp
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    return p / s


def make_max_p(c, M):
    """Uniform weight on the near-maximal convolution coefficients."""
    m = c.size
    tol = 1e-10 + 0.02 * M
    idx = np.where(c >= M - tol)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=np.int64)
    elif idx.size > max(16, m // 8):
        k = int(min(idx.size, max(16, m // 8)))
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    p = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
    p[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
    return p


def make_excess_p(c, focus):
    """Weight proportional to excess over focus, if there is excess."""
    excess = np.maximum(c - focus, 0.0)
    s = float(excess.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return excess / s


def try_updates_for_p(a, p, cur, etas):
    """Try several multiplicative gradient steps for one subgradient p."""
    g = 2.0 * correlate_valid(p, a)
    if g.size != a.size:
        return None, cur
    g = g - float(np.mean(g))
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
        return None, cur

    best_h = None
    best_sc = cur
    for eta in etas:
        eta = float(eta)
        h = a * np.exp(-eta * np.clip(g / scale, -25.0, 25.0))
        h = normalize_nonneg(h + 1e-12)
        sc = score_internal(h)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_h = h
    return best_h, best_sc


def general_subgradient_step(a, c, M, temp_frac, target_score, cur):
    """
    Combine several subgradient directions:
      1. smoothed max (softmax),
      2. hinge/focus around a target coefficient level,
      3. exact max active set.
    Returns a sequence with lower internal score, or None.
    """
    n = a.size
    best_h = None
    best_sc = cur

    # 1. Smoothed max.
    p = make_softmax_p(c, M, max(1e-20, float(temp_frac) * M))
    if p is not None:
        if n <= 1024:
            etas = (0.05, 0.12, 0.25, 0.50)
        else:
            etas = (0.06, 0.18)
        h, sc = try_updates_for_p(a, p, cur, etas)
        if h is not None:
            best_h = h
            best_sc = sc

    # 2. Focus/hinge relative to the desired target score.
    if target_score is not None and float(target_score) > 0.0:
        target_M = float(target_score) / (2.0 * float(n))
        focus = max(target_M, M - 0.10 * M)
        p = make_excess_p(c, focus)
        if p is not None:
            if n <= 1024:
                etas = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20)
            else:
                etas = (0.02, 0.08)
            h, sc = try_updates_for_p(a, p, best_sc, etas)
            if h is not None:
                best_h = h
                best_sc = sc

    # 3. Exact max active-set step.
    p = make_max_p(c, M)
    if p is not None:
        if n <= 1024:
            etas = (0.01, 0.02, 0.05)
        else:
            etas = (0.01, 0.03)
        h, sc = try_updates_for_p(a, p, best_sc, etas)
        if h is not None:
            best_h = h
            best_sc = sc

    return best_h


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# LP direction
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def choose_active_rows(c, n):
    """Select important convolution constraints for the LP."""
    m = c.size
    if n <= 768:
        K = m
    elif n <= 1536:
        K = 1024
    elif n <= 3072:
        K = 600
    else:
        K = 300
    K = int(max(1, min(m, K)))
    if K >= m:
        return np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)

    top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    mi = int(np.argmax(c))
    half = max(1, K // 4)
    l = max(0, mi - half)
    r = min(m, mi + half + 1)
    win = np.arange(l, r, dtype=np.int64)

    rows = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, win)))
    if rows.size <= K:
        return rows.astype(np.int64)

    order = np.argsort(c[rows])
    return rows[order[-K:]].astype(np.int64)


def build_active_matrix(f, rows):
    """
    Matrix A for constraints f*g <= M.
    Row k: (A[row, :])_j = f[k-j] when 0 <= k-j < n(f).
    """
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = f.size
    rows = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1)
    A = np.zeros((rows.size, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r, k in enumerate(rows):
        k = int(k)
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n, k + 1)
        if hi > lo:
            idx = np.arange(lo, hi, dtype=np.int64)
            A[r, idx] = f[k - idx]
    return A


def solve_lp_direction(f, M, rows):
    """
    LP relaxation direction:
        maximize sum(g)
        subject to  A(g) <= 1  after normalizing by M,
                    0 <= g <= 1.
    The upper bound 1 prevents degeneracy/unboundedness and is harmless
    because the returned vector is rescaled to sum 1.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = f.size
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return None

    try:
        A = build_active_matrix(f, rows)
    except Exception:
        return None

    if A.size == 0 or not np.any(A):
        return None

    A = A / M
    ccoef = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bvec = np.ones(A.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = (0.0, 1.0)

    try:
        res = linprog(
            ccoef,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=bvec,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                ccoef,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=bvec,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res is not None and res.status == 0 and res.x is not None:
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        s = float(g.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-12:
            return g
    return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Initial constructions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def load_provided_sequence():
    """Load the supplied global construction, if present, and clean it."""
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def make_power_alpha_gamma(N, alpha, gamma, eps=0.5):
    """
    a_i ∝ (i + 0.5 + eps)^(-alpha) exp(gamma * i/(N-1)).
    For alpha=0.5 and gamma=0 this approximates the classic 1/sqrt(i) profile.
    """
    i = np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64)
    base = (i + eps + 0.5) ** (-float(alpha))
    if N > 1:
        t = i / float(N - 1)
        base = base * np.exp(float(gamma) * t)
    base = base + 1e-12
    return normalize_nonneg(base)


def make_central_binomial(N):
    """
    b_i = binom(2i,i) / 4^i, normalized.
    Its generating function is truncated (1-z)^(-1/2); the infinite sequence
    has square convolution coefficients equal to 1 for positive lags.
    """
    b = np.ones(max(1, int(N)), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, b.size):
        b[k] = b[k - 1] * (2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k)
    return normalize_nonneg(b)


def make_central_binomial_gamma(N, gamma):
    base = make_central_binomial(N)
    if N > 1:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)
        base = base * np.exp(float(gamma) * t)
    return normalize_nonneg(base + 1e-12)


def make_mirrored_sequence(N, w):
    """Blend a central-binomial profile with its mirror."""
    base = make_central_binomial(N)
    rev = base[::-1].copy()
    a = (1.0 - float(w)) * base + float(w) * rev
    return normalize_nonneg(a + 1e-12)


def make_central_plus_tail(N, w):
    """Central-binomial profile plus a small right-half plateau."""
    a = make_central_binomial(N).copy()
    block = max(1, N // 2)
    a[block:] += float(w) / float(block)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def make_base_plus_tail(N, w):
    """One-sided power profile plus a small right-half plateau."""
    a = make_power_alpha_gamma(N, 0.5, 0.0).copy()
    block = max(1, N // 2)
    a[block:] += float(w) / float(block)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def grid_score_key(item):
    return item[1]


def generate_grid_candidates(N, rng, keep=10):
    """Generate a diverse set of strong nonnegative starts."""
    cands = []

    # Central-binomial and its simple right-side enrichments.
    base = make_central_binomial(N)
    cands.append(base)
    cands.append(normalize_nonneg(base[::-1] + 1e-12))

    for gamma in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0):
        cands.append(make_central_binomial_gamma(N, gamma))

    for w in (0.02, 0.05, 0.08, 0.12, 0.18, 0.25, 0.35):
        cands.append(make_mirrored_sequence(N, w))
        cands.append(make_central_plus_tail(N, w))
        cands.append(make_base_plus_tail(N, w))

    # Power/exponential profiles.
    alphas = (0.2, 0.28, 0.35, 0.4, 0.45, 0.48, 0.5)
    gammas = (-3.0, -2.0, -1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0)
    for alpha in alphas:
        for gamma in gammas:
            cands.append(make_power_alpha_gamma(N, alpha, gamma))

    # A few smooth profiles and random-smooth starts for basin diversity.
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)
    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        a = np.power(np.sin(0.5 * np.pi * x), p)
        cands.append(normalize_nonneg(a + 1e-12))

    for _ in range(4):
        base = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=N)
        wlen = max(3, min(N, N // 40 + 1))
        w = np.ones(wlen, dtype=np.float64)
        w /= w.sum()
        base = np.convolve(base, w, mode="same")
        a = np.exp(0.35 * base)
        cands.append(normalize_nonneg(a))

    scored = []
    for a in cands:
        s = score_internal(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((a, s))
    scored.sort(key=grid_score_key)
    return [a for (a, _s) in scored[:max(1, int(keep))]]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Search bookkeeping
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def candidate_score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def phase_task_score_key(task):
    return task[0]


def add_phase_task(phases, phase, seq, base_time):
    if seq is None:
        return
    seq = normalize_nonneg(seq)
    if seq.size < 2:
        return
    sc = score_internal(seq)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    phase = int(max(0, min(2, phase)))
    tasks = phases[phase]
    # Keep the task list bounded; prefer lower-scored tasks.
    if len(tasks) >= 14:
        tasks.sort(key=phase_task_score_key)
        if sc >= tasks[-1][0] + 1e-12:
            return
    tasks.append((float(sc), seq, float(max(1.0, base_time)), phase))


def update_state(state, seq, score):
    """Update the global best internal score and candidate pool."""
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return False
    seq = normalize_nonneg(seq)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return False

    old = state["score"]
    if score < old - 1e-10:
        state["score"] = float(score)
        state["seq"] = seq.copy()
        state["candidates"].append((float(score), seq.copy()))
        state["candidates"].sort(key=candidate_score_key)
        if len(state["candidates"]) > 16:
            del state["candidates"][16:]
        print(f"update_best score={state['score']:.12f} n={seq.size}", flush=True)
        return True
    return False


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Local optimizer
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def local_search(a, time_limit, rng, state):
    """Local search from a normalized nonnegative sequence."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size

    if n < 32:
        a = resize_to_length(a, 64)
        n = a.size
    if n > 4096:
        a = resize_to_length(a, 4096)
        n = a.size

    a = normalize_nonneg(a + 1e-12)

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_internal(best)
    update_state(state, best, best_sc)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(10.0, float(time_limit))

    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    temp_frac = 0.25
    lp_attempts = 0

    if n <= 512:
        max_lp_attempts = 220
    elif n <= 1024:
        max_lp_attempts = 100
    elif n <= 2048:
        max_lp_attempts = 55
    else:
        max_lp_attempts = 22

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1

        c = conv_fft(a)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        cur = 2.0 * float(n) * M

        if cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = a.copy()
            best_sc = cur
            update_state(state, best, best_sc)
            no_improve = 0
            temp_frac = max(0.02, temp_frac * 0.94)
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if state["score"] < state["target_score"]:
            state["target_score"] = max(1.30, state["score"] - 0.002)

        if no_improve > 35:
            temp_frac = min(0.50, temp_frac * 1.25)
            if no_improve > 70:
                a = random_perturb(a, rng, 0.18)
                no_improve = 0
                continue

        # LP direction, if available.
        if linprog is not None and n <= 4096:
            if n <= 512:
                every = 1
            elif n <= 1024:
                every = 2
            elif n <= 2048:
                every = 4
            else:
                every = 8

            if lp_attempts < max_lp_attempts and (it % every == 0):
                rows = choose_active_rows(c, n)
                M_lp = M * (0.95 if no_improve > 15 else 1.0)

                g = solve_lp_direction(a, M_lp, rows)
                lp_attempts += 1
                if (g is None or float(np.sum(g)) <= 1.0 + 1e-5) and M_lp < M:
                    g = solve_lp_direction(a, M, rows)
                    lp_attempts += 1

                if g is not None:
                    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
                    Sg = float(g.sum())
                    if Sg > 1.0 + 1e-5:
                        gn = g / Sg
                        best_h = a
                        best_cur = cur

                        if Sg > 1.25:
                            ts = (0.01, 0.03, 0.08, 0.15, 0.30, 0.50)
                        else:
                            ts = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35)

                        for t in ts:
                            t = float(t)
                            h = (1.0 - t) * a + t * gn
                            h = normalize_nonneg(h + 1e-12)
                            sc = score_internal(h)
                            if sc < best_cur:
                                best_cur = sc
                                best_h = h

                        if best_cur < cur - 1e-12:
                            a = best_h
                            continue

        # Subgradient / multiplicative projected step.
        h = general_subgradient_step(
            a, c, M, temp_frac, state["target_score"], cur
        )
        if h is not None:
            a = normalize_nonneg(h + 1e-12)
            continue

        # Small escape move.
        a = random_perturb(a, rng, 0.04)

    return best, best_sc


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Finalization with the official evaluator
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def official_score(seq):
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)]
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")

    for v in vals:
        if not np.isfinite(v) or v < 0.0:
            return float("inf")

    try:
        s = evaluate_sequence(vals)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")

    s = float(s)
    return s if np.isfinite(s) else float("inf")


def finalize_official(state, provided, deadline):
    """
    Evaluate a small pool of strong internal candidates with the official
    evaluator and return the best valid list.
    """
    seqs = []

    if state["seq"] is not None:
        seqs.append(normalize_nonneg(state["seq"]))

    for item in state["candidates"]:
        seqs.append(normalize_nonneg(item[1]))

    if provided is not None:
        seqs.append(normalize_nonneg(provided))

    if state["seq"] is not None:
        for ns in (512, 1024, 2048, 4096):
            seqs.append(resize_to_length(state["seq"], ns))

    # Guaranteed fallback.
    seqs.append(np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64))

    scored = []
    for arr in seqs:
        if arr is None:
            continue
        arr = normalize_nonneg(arr)
        if arr.size < 2 or arr.size > 4096:
            continue
        sc = score_internal(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((arr, sc))

    scored.sort(key=grid_score_key)

    best_seq = [1.0] * 512
    best_off = float("inf")

    # Official convolution is O(n^2); cap the number of long evaluations.
    evaluated_long = 0
    for idx, (arr, _s) in enumerate(scored):
        if idx >= 24:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        arr = normalize_nonneg(arr)
        if arr.size > 4096:
            continue

        if arr.size >= 2048:
            evaluated_long += 1
            if evaluated_long > 6:
                continue

        vals = [float(x) for x in arr]
        off = official_score(vals)
        if off < best_off:
            best_off = off
            best_seq = vals
            print(f"official_best score={best_off:.12f} n={len(best_seq)}", flush=True)

    return best_seq


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for the best valid sequence of non-negative heights.

    Parameters are intentionally permissive; the grader may call this with
    no arguments or with keyword arguments.
    """
    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 10.0:
        budget = 1800.0

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    # Reserve time for final official evaluation and validation.
    hard_deadline = start_time + max(60.0, budget - 90.0)

    state = {
        "score": float("inf"),
        "seq": None,
        "candidates": [],
        "target_score": 1.50,
    }

    provided = load_provided_sequence()
    if provided is not None:
        s = score_internal(provided)
        update_state(state, provided, s)

    phases = [[], [], []]

    # Provided construction is historically strong; try several lengths.
    if provided is not None:
        add_phase_task(phases, 0, resize_to_length(provided, 512), 70.0)
        add_phase_task(phases, 1, resize_to_length(provided, 1024), 90.0)
        add_phase_task(phases, 2, resize_to_length(provided, 2048), 110.0)
        add_phase_task(phases, 2, resize_to_length(provided, 4096), 80.0)

    # Fast grid at 512 to discover strong basins.
    try:
        grid = generate_grid_candidates(512, rng, keep=12)
    except Exception:
        grid = []

    for seq in grid:
        add_phase_task(phases, 0, seq, 45.0)

    # Always have something to run.
    if sum(len(t) for t in phases) == 0:
        add_phase_task(phases, 0, make_central_binomial(512), 60.0)

    # Process phases by increasing length. Later phases receive upscaled
    # candidates from earlier successes.
    for phase in range(3):
        tasks = phases[phase]
        if not tasks:
            continue

        tasks.sort(key=phase_task_score_key)
        max_tasks = 8 if phase == 0 else (5 if phase == 1 else 4)
        if len(tasks) > max_tasks:
            tasks = tasks[:max_tasks]

        for task in tasks:
            remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
            if remaining < 45.0:
                break

            sc, seq, btime, pnum = task
            limit = min(float(btime), max(10.0, remaining - 30.0))
            if limit < 18.0:
                continue

            # Do not spend much time on clearly bad starts once we have a good one.
            if np.isfinite(state["score"]) and sc > state["score"] + 0.12:
                limit = min(limit, 25.0)

            cur_seq = normalize_nonneg(seq)
            if pnum == 2 and cur_seq.size > 4096:
                cur_seq = resize_to_length(cur_seq, 4096)

            try:
                local_search(cur_seq, limit, rng, state)
            except Exception:
                pass

            if state["seq"] is not None and phase < 2 and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 120.0:
                if phase == 0:
                    add_phase_task(phases, 1, resize_to_length(state["seq"], 1024), 90.0)
                else:
                    add_phase_task(phases, 2, resize_to_length(state["seq"], 2048), 100.0)
                    add_phase_task(phases, 2, resize_to_length(state["seq"], 4096), 70.0)

        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 60.0:
            break

    # Final refinement: upscale best if possible, otherwise polish same length.
    same_no_gain = 0
    while time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 45.0:
        if state["seq"] is None:
            break

        cur_n = state["seq"].size
        if cur_n < 1024:
            nxt = 1024
        elif cur_n < 2048:
            nxt = 2048
        elif cur_n < 4096:
            nxt = 4096
        else:
            nxt = 4096

        remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 75.0:
            break

        limit = min(90.0, max(20.0, remaining - 30.0))
        old = state["score"]

        seq = resize_to_length(state["seq"], nxt)
        try:
            local_search(seq, limit, rng, state)
        except Exception:
            pass

        if nxt == cur_n and state["score"] >= old - 1e-9:
            same_no_gain += 1
            if same_no_gain >= 2:
                break
        else:
            same_no_gain = 0

    # Absolute fallback: any constant sequence is valid.
    if state["seq"] is None:
        state["score"] = 2.0
        state["seq"] = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        final_seq = finalize_official(state, provided, hard_deadline)
    except Exception:
        final_seq = [float(x) for x in normalize_nonneg(state["seq"])]

    # Sanitize the returned list.
    if not isinstance(final_seq, list):
        final_seq = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(final_seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)]

    if not final_seq:
        final_seq = [1.0] * 512

    out = []
    for x in final_seq:
        try:
            fx = float(x)
        except Exception:
            fx = 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(fx) or fx < 0.0:
            fx = 0.0
        if fx > 1000.0:
            fx = 1000.0
        out.append(fx)

    if not out or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [1.0] * 512

    return out
```
